# API Surface

_Living artifact. Initial seed by `/andromeda-route` Phase 6 from arch's Standard Contracts. Regenerated by `/wrap-session` Phase 5 reconcile from actual code state after each session ends (per `integrity-protocol.md` Part A + Part B)._
<!-- METADATA start -->
**Last reconciled:** 2026-05-13T17:12:22Z
**Tooling:** `for crate in crates/*; do (cd "$crate" && cargo +nightly public-api --simplified); done` (per-crate; cargo-public-api 0.51 requires nightly + does not support workspace-level invocation)
**Source:** arch.md §Standard Contracts (IPC methods / HTTP endpoints / event topics / schema files / public exports) + §Occupied Resources — seed; actual code via `cargo public-api` per-crate iteration — reconcile
**Maintenance:** wrap-session Phase 5. **2026-05-13 wrap session 61:** chunk #51 (Smoke tests + tauri-driver matrix; narrowed scope per /implement Path A') landed — public-API delta zero. The chunk added 2 new files in xtask (`xtask/src/smoke.rs` + `xtask/src/bundle_format.rs`) + extended xtask/src/main.rs с `Cmd::Smoke` variant. xtask is а binary crate excluded by tooling scope (`cargo public-api` iterates `crates/*` only); same exclusion as chunk #50's pulse-app additions. No `crates/*` library crate was modified by chunk #51. Net delta vs session 60 baseline (4934 lines): 0 lines = 4934 lines. **2026-05-13 wrap session 60:** chunk #50 (E2E test pass — P1-P7) landed — public-API delta zero. The chunk added pulse-app/src/lib.rs (`pub mod` re-exports of existing internal modules + `pub fn taurpc_export_config`) + 9 new integration test files in pulse-app/tests/. None surface in `cargo public-api` per-crate iteration over `crates/*` — pulse-app is а binary crate excluded by tooling scope; integration tests аre not public-API. observability::init + heartbeat::spawn elevated from `pub(crate)` к `pub` to cross the lib/bin boundary; same exclusion applies (pulse-app modules не covered). Net delta vs session 59 baseline (4934 lines): 0 lines = 4934 lines. **2026-05-12 wrap session 59:** chunk #49 (MCP tool methods + IPC routers) public-API delta surfaces. New public items in `mcp-server` crate: `pub mod tools` + 4 per-tool argument structs (`QueryTracesArgs` / `QueryMetricsArgs` / `QueryLogsArgs` / `GenerateSnapshotArgs`) + 4 dispatch functions + 4 tool-name constants (`TOOL_QUERY_TRACES` / `TOOL_QUERY_METRICS` / `TOOL_QUERY_LOGS` / `TOOL_GENERATE_SNAPSHOT`) + `ALL_TOOL_NAMES` slice + top-level `dispatch_tool` entry point + helpers (`budget_for_count`); 2 new `contract::Error` variants (`ToolDispatchFailed` / `ToolArgsInvalid`); `jsonrpc::tools_list_with_4_tools` fn. Net delta vs session 58 baseline (4861 lines): +73 lines = 4934 lines, distributed under the `mcp-server` crate's public-API block. Note: `pulse-app/src/mcp_router.rs` DTOs (`McpServerState` / `McpStatusDto` / `McpStartResult` / `McpStopResult`) live in the binary crate and DO NOT surface in `cargo public-api` per-crate iteration (binary crates excluded by tooling); same for the sidecar binary's `SidecarContext` struct in `bin/andromeda-pulse-mcp.rs`. The `crates/ui-bridge/src/contract.rs` `From<McpServerError> for AppError` impl rewired for the 2 new variants but the public surface (AppError variants) is unchanged — net 0 line delta from ui-bridge. **2026-05-12 wrap session 58:** chunk #48 (rmcp stdio sidecar + double-gate) public-API delta surfaces. New public items in `mcp-server` crate: `contract::Error::{FeatureNotEnabled { reason: &'static str }, EnvVarDisabled, RmcpInit { detail: String }, JsonRpcFraming { detail: String }, Io { source: std::io::Error }, TracingInit { detail: String }}` (replaces `Placeholder` from session 57 baseline); `feature_gate::{GateState enum + Enabled/EnvDisabled/FeatureMissing variants, ENV_MCP_ENABLED const, feature_flag_enabled fn, env_var_mcp_enabled fn, validate_double_gate fn}`; `tracing_setup::{SERVICE_NAME const, init fn}`; `jsonrpc::{JSONRPC_VERSION const, MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION const, CODE_PARSE_ERROR / CODE_INVALID_REQUEST / CODE_METHOD_NOT_FOUND / CODE_INVALID_PARAMS / CODE_INTERNAL_ERROR consts, Request struct + jsonrpc/id/method/params fields, SuccessResponse / ErrorResponse / ErrorObject structs, parse_request / success / error / sanitize_error_data / initialize_result / empty_tools_list fns}`. Net delta vs session 57 baseline (4745 lines): +116 lines = 4861 lines, all under the `mcp-server` crate's public-API block. Note: `crates/mcp-server/src/bin/andromeda-pulse-mcp.rs` binary entry does NOT surface in `cargo public-api` per-crate iteration (binary crates excluded by tooling). `crates/ui-bridge/src/contract.rs` From<McpServerError> impl rewired for the 6 new variants but the public surface (AppError variants) is unchanged — net 0 line delta from ui-bridge. **2026-05-12 wrap session 57:** chunk #47 (Plugin loader + IPC routers) public-API delta surfaces. New public items in `plugins` crate: `loader::{PluginRegistry struct + empty/loaded_count/active_invocations/record_invocation/plugins/find/replace_plugins methods, LoadedPlugin struct + id/category/component/basename/byte_count fields, resolve_plugin_dir/canonicalize_plugin_dir/discover_plugins fns, declared_exports fn}`; `contract::Error::{PathCanonicalizationFailed, LoadFailed, NotFound variants}`; `contract::PluginCategory` gains `Hash` impl; `contract::heartbeat_payload` signature changed from `() -> PluginsHeartbeat` to `(&PluginRegistry) -> PluginsHeartbeat`. Net delta vs session 56 baseline (4715 lines): +30 lines = 4745 lines, all under the `plugins` crate's public-API block. Note: `pulse-app/src/plugins_router.rs` DTOs (`PluginDto`, `PluginListEnvelope`, `PluginInvokeResult`) live in the binary crate and DO NOT surface in `cargo public-api` per-crate iteration (binary crates excluded by tooling). **2026-05-12 wrap session 56:** chunk #46 (capability sandbox + ResourceLimiter) public-API delta surfaces. New public items: `plugins::sandbox::{ResourceLimiterState struct, DEFAULT_MEMORY_CAP_BYTES, DEFAULT_TABLE_ELEMENTS_CAP, DEFAULT_INSTANCES_CAP consts, for_category/memory_growing/table_growing/instances/tables/memories methods via ResourceLimiter impl, store_for_category fn}`; `plugins::capability::{linker_for_category fn}`; `plugins::contract::Error::{ResourceLimitExceeded, CapabilityRejected variants}`. LIVING-block content REWRITTEN this wrap per v2.1 format-mismatch reconciliation discipline: prior LIVING content had build-status messages ("Compiling proc-macro2", "Finished `dev` profile") mixed with API surface text (5526 lines total, build noise interleaved); fresh tooling output captured stdout-only (4702 lines, clean `pub mod`/`pub fn`/`impl` declarations). Net delta after format fix: 5526 → 4715 lines (-811 lines = -14.7%) reflecting (a) build-noise removal + (b) new public items from chunk #46. **2026-05-11 wrap session 55:** no-op verification — source code in `crates/` unchanged since session 54 wrap (no chunk implementation this session; only mtime-refresh edits in `.claude/` + `.andromeda/`). Per Phase 5 step 5 "diff empty AND last_reconciled <24h ago: no-op + timestamp refresh." Public-API stable at session 54 baseline (5526 lines under LIVING markers). 2026-05-11 wrap session 54 + earlier maintenance notes preserved in git history at `.andromeda/context/api-surface.md` prior commits.
